\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}
, this implies that

(7.1.6)

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X},\qquad J\in\operatorname{ob}(G).

We now compute

\begin{split}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern &=\bigcup_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\,\bigcup_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}\bigl(({\mathcal{V}}(w))({\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X})\cap({\mathcal{V}}(w))({\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X})\bigr)\\
&=\bigcup_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\,\bigcup_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}\bigl(({\mathcal{V}}(w))({\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X})\cap{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}\bigr)\\
&={\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}({\mathcal{V}}(w))({\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X})={\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

(7.1.7)

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}={\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}.

We leave open the questions of whether
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}
is independent of
X
and whether

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}
is a cone.

Proposition 7.2
.

For any object
J
of the groupoid
G
, we have
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}=\mathcal{C}\cap J^{\perp}
where

\mathcal{C}:=\{\,v\in V\mid B(v,\alpha)\geq 0\text{ \rm for all $\alpha\in\Pi$}\,\}
.

Proof.

Let
x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
. Then
x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}=J^{\perp}
. Hence
B(x,J)\subseteq{\{0\}}
. Also by definition of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
, we have

B(x,\pi_{J}(\alpha))\geq 0
for all
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
.
Since
\pi_{J}(\alpha)\in\alpha+\operatorname{span}(J)
where
B(x,\operatorname{span}(J))=\{0\}
, this gives
B(x,\alpha)\geq 0
for all
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
. Hence
B(x,\Pi)\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
and
x\in\mathcal{C}\cap J^{\perp}
. This proves

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}\subseteq\mathcal{C}\cap J^{\perp}
.

To prove the reverse inclusion, let
x\in\mathcal{C}\cap J^{\perp}
. Then
x\in J^{\perp}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. Further, since
x\in\mathcal{C}
, we have

B(x,\Pi\setminus J)\subseteq B(x,\Pi)\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
. Hence

B(x,\alpha)\geq 0
for all
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
.
But since
\pi_{J}(\alpha)\in\alpha+\operatorname{span}(J)
and
x\in J^{\perp}
, it follows that
B(x,\alpha)=B(x,\pi_{J}(\alpha))
for all
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
. Thus, we get

B(x,\pi_{J}(\alpha))\geq 0
for all
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
,
so we deduce that
x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
. This proves that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\mathcal{C}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}=\mathcal{C}\cap J^{\perp}
.
∎

7.3.

Let
J
be an object of the groupoid
G
. Let
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}=J^{\perp}
. Define

A_{X,J}(v):=\{\,\beta\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}_{X}^{\mathrm{re},+}\mid B_{J}(v,\beta)<0\,\}.

Lemma 7.4
.

Let
J\in\operatorname{ob}(G)
.

(a)

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}\subseteq\{\,v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}\mid|A_{X,J}(v)|<\infty\,\}
.

(b)

\{\,v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}_{X}\mid|A_{X,J}(v)|<\infty\,\}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
.

Proof.

We prove (a).
Let
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}
. By definition, there exists a morphism
g=(J,w,K)
in
G
and an element
v^{\prime}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}
such that

v=({\mathcal{V}}(g))(v^{\prime})
. We shall show that

A_{X,J}(v)\subseteq\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X,g}
, where the right hand side is the

\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}
-inversion set of
g
. Suppose that

\alpha\in A_{X,J}(v)
.
Then

0>B_{J}(\alpha,v)=B_{K}(({\mathcal{V}}(g^{-1}))(\alpha),({\mathcal{V}}(g^{-1}))(v))=B_{J}((\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}(g^{-1}))(\alpha),v^{\prime}).

Since
v^{\prime}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}
,
we must have
(\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}(g^{-1}))(\alpha)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-10.17876pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-10.17876pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.88959pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.88959pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{-}
. Since
\alpha\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{+}_{X}
by
definition of
A_{X,J}(v)
,
this shows that
\alpha\in\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X,g}
and hence

A_{X,J}(v)\subseteq\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X,g}
.
Now we note that

\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X,g}=\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X,g}\cong\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X,g}=\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\mathrm{re}}}_{X,g}

where the bijection is from Theorem
1.49
. Using Corollary
4.5
, we see that
\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X,g}
is finite, and hence so is
A_{X,J}(v)
.
This competes the proof of (a).

To prove (b), we show by induction on
n
that if
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}_{X}^{{\mathrm{im}}}
and
A_{X,J}(v)
is finite of cardinality
n
, then
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
.
If
n=0
, then
A_{X,J}(v)={\emptyset}
and

v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}

by the definitions. Suppose
n>0
, say
\alpha\in A_{X,J}
. We may write

\alpha=\sum_{\beta\in\Gamma}c_{\beta}\beta
where

\Gamma
is a finite subset of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}
and
c_{\beta}\in\mathbb{R}_{>0}
for all
\beta\in\Gamma
. Since

B_{J}(\alpha,v)=\sum_{\beta\in\Gamma}c_{\beta}B_{J}(\beta,v)<0
, we have
B_{J}(\beta,v)<0
for some
\beta\in\Gamma
. We can’t have
\beta\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}
, since

v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}
. Hence
\beta\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}\cap A_{X,J}(v)
.

Write
\beta=\pi_{J}(\gamma)=\gamma_{J}
where
\gamma\in\Pi
. Let
w=\nu(\gamma,J)
,
K:=wJ
and define
\delta\in\Pi
by
J\cup\{\gamma\}=K\cup\{\delta\}
, so
w^{-1}=\nu(\delta,K)
. We have a morphism
g:=(K,w,J)
of
G
,
with
(\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}(g))(\beta)=(\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}(g))(\gamma_{J})=-\delta_{K}
.

Write
\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}(g)
as
w
for notational simplicity.
Let
v^{\prime}:=w(v)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}
.
We claim that

(7.4.1)

A_{X,K}(v^{\prime})=w(A_{X,J}(v)\setminus\operatorname{span}(\beta))

To prove this, note first that
B_{K}(\delta_{K},v^{\prime})=B_{K}(-w\beta,wv)=-B_{J}(\beta,v)>0
so
\operatorname{span}(\delta_{K})=w(\operatorname{span}(\beta))
is disjoint from
A_{X,K}(v^{\prime})
.
There is a bijection

\rho\mapsto w^{-1}\rho\colon\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-10.17876pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-10.17876pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.88959pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.88959pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{+}\setminus\operatorname{span}(\delta_{K})\xrightarrow{\cong}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{+}\setminus\operatorname{span}(\beta).

This holds, for instance, because the inversion set for
\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{X}
of the Brink-Howlett generator
g
is the parallelism class of roots containing
\delta_{K}
(by Theorem
1.22
and Proposition
3.8
)
and that parallelism classes is as described in Proposition
6.15
.
The claim follows, since for
\rho\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-10.17876pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-10.17876pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.88959pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.88959pt{K}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{+}\setminus\operatorname{span}(\delta_{K})
, we have

\begin{split}&B_{K}(\rho,v^{\prime})<0\iff B_{K}(\rho,wv)<0\\
\iff&B_{J}(w^{-1}\rho,w^{-1}v^{\prime})<0\iff B_{J}(w^{-1}\rho,v)<0.\end{split}

Now in the claim,
\beta\in A_{X,J}
, so
|A_{X,K}(v^{\prime})|<|A_{X,J}|
. Also,
v^{\prime}=w(v)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}
since
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}}
. It follows by the inductive hypothesis that
v^{\prime}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, from which
v=wv^{\prime}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
follows using the definitions.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.65
.

By (
7.1.7
) and Lemma
7.4
, we have

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}_{X}^{{\mathrm{im}}}\subseteq\{\,v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}_{X}^{{\mathrm{im}}}\mid|A_{X,J}(v)|<\infty\,\}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}},

which implies Theorem
1.65
(a)–(b). Part (c) follows readily using the fact that
{\mathcal{V}}
is a functor. It remains to prove (d). It is clear that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
is a cone, by its definition. The fact that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
is a cone follows from its description in (a).
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.67
.

Part (a) follows from Proposition
7.2
.

Since
F:=\Pi\cap A^{\perp}
and
A\subseteq C
, part (b) follows from Proposition
2.18
.

For part (c), let
K,L
be objects of
H
. Let
(L,w,K)
be a morphism of
G^{\prime}
. Then
w\in W_{F}
, and since
W_{F}
stabilizes
A
pointwise, we conclude that
{\mathcal{V}}(L,w,K)
stabilizes
A
pointwise. Thus,
(L,w,K)
is a morphism of
H
. Conversely, if
(L,w,K)
is a morphism of
H
, then
{\mathcal{V}}(L,w,K)
fixes
A
pointwise, so by part (b),
w\in W_{F}
, and thus
w
is a morphism of
G^{\prime}
. This proves (c).

For part (d), let
K
be an object of
H
. By definition of
H
, we have
A\subseteq K^{\perp}
. Note that
K
is also an object of the full subgroupoid
G^{\prime}
. Let
L
be another object of
G^{\prime}
that lies in the same component as
K
. Since
L
and
K
lie in the same component of
G^{\prime}
, it follows that there exists a morphism
(L,w,K)
of
G^{\prime}
where
w\in W_{F}
.

We claim that
L\subseteq A^{\perp}
. Let
\beta\in L
. There exists
\alpha\in K
such that

\beta=w(\alpha)
.
Since
F=\Pi\cap A^{\perp}
, we have
\operatorname{span}(F)\subseteq A^{\perp}
. Also,
\alpha\in K\subseteq A^{\perp}
since
A\subseteq K^{\perp}
.
Hence
\beta=w(\alpha)\in\alpha+\operatorname{span}(F)\subseteq A^{\perp}
, for all
\beta\in L
. Thus,
L\subseteq A^{\perp}
as claimed.

This claim implies that
A\subseteq L^{\perp}
, so
L
is an object of
H
. We have shown that if
L
is an object of
G^{\prime}
that lies in the same component of
G^{\prime}
as some object
K
(where
K
is an object of
H
), then
L
is also an object of
H
. This fact along with what we proved in part (c) proves that
H
is a union of components of
G^{\prime}
.
∎

8.
Imaginary cones of realized Brink-Howlett groupoids

8.1.

Define
{\mathcal{K}}:=(-{\mathcal{C}})\cap\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)\subseteq V
and
{\mathcal{Z}}:=\bigcup_{w\in W}w({\mathcal{K}})\subseteq V
.

Proposition 8.2
.

If
J
is an object of
G
, then

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}\subseteq{\mathcal{K}}
.

Proof.

Let
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}=(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}})\cap\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}})
. Then
v\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}=-({\mathcal{C}}\cap J^{\perp})\subseteq-{\mathcal{C}}
, by Proposition
7.2
.
We also have
v\in\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}})
where
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}=\pi_{J}(\Pi\setminus J)
. For
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
, we have

\pi_{J}(\alpha)=\alpha-\sum_{\beta\in J}B(\alpha,\beta)\omega_{\beta}^{J}
by Proposition
6.6
. Now by Theorem
2.9
,
B(\alpha,\beta)\leq 0
for
\beta\in J
, since
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
, and
\omega_{\beta}^{J}\in\operatorname{cone}(J)\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)
by
6.7
. Hence

\pi_{J}(\alpha)\in\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)
. It follows that
v\in\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)
. Hence
v\in-{\mathcal{C}}\cap\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)={\mathcal{K}}
.
Thus, we have proven that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}\subseteq{\mathcal{K}}
.
∎

Proposition 8.3
.

Let
J
be an object of
G
. Then

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}\subseteq{\mathcal{Z}}
.

Proof.

By definition of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}
and Proposition
8.2
, we have

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}=\bigcup_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\,\bigcup_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}({\mathcal{V}}(w))(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}})\subseteq\bigcup_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\,\bigcup_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}w{\mathcal{K}}\subseteq\bigcup_{w\in W}w{\mathcal{K}}={\mathcal{Z}}.

∎

Proof of Theorem
1.69
(a)–(c).

Part (a) follows readily from the definitions using the fact that
{\mathcal{V}}
is a functor.

We prove (b).Since
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}}\subseteq-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
, the definition of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}
gives

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}=\bigcup_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\,\bigcup_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}({\mathcal{V}}(w))(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}})\subseteq\bigcup_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\,\bigcup_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}({\mathcal{V}}(w))(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}})=-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}.

Thus,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}\subseteq-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
.

Now by Proposition
8.3
and
[
25
, Proposition 3.2(a)]
, we have
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}Z^{{\kern-5.55pt\kern 5.93889pt}}_{{\kern-9.2384pt{J}\kern 5.93889pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}Z^{{\kern-5.55pt\kern 5.93889pt}}_{{\kern-9.2384pt{J}\kern 5.93889pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}Z^{{\kern-3.30652pt\kern 3.6954pt}}_{{\kern-6.08394pt{J}\kern 3.6954pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}Z^{{\kern-2.25069pt\kern 2.63957pt}}_{{\kern-5.0281pt{J}\kern 2.63957pt}}}\subseteq{\mathcal{Z}}
. By
[
25
, Proposition 3.2(a)]
, we have

{\mathcal{Z}}=(-{\mathcal{X}})\cap\big(\bigcap_{w\in W}w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi))\big)
.
Hence,

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}\subseteq{\mathcal{Z}}\subseteq\bigcap_{w\in W}w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi))\subseteq\bigcap_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\bigcap_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi))

Thus,

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}\subseteq(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\big(\bigcap_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\bigcap_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi))\big)

But since
-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}\subseteq J^{\perp}=w(K^{\perp})
for
w
and
K
as in the intersection, this gives

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}\subseteq(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\big(\bigcap_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\bigcap_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}(w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi))\cap w(K^{\perp})\big).

By Proposition
1.50
(b), we have

w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi))\cap w(K^{\perp})=w(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)\cap K^{\perp})\subseteq w(\pi_{K}(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)))=w(\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})).

Therefore,

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}\subseteq(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\big(\bigcap_{K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)}\bigcap_{w\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.04243pt\kern 5.3202pt}}_{{\kern-7.81985pt{J}\kern 5.3202pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.47409pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-4.60081pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-1.68785pt\kern 1.96562pt}}_{{\kern-3.81458pt{J}\kern 1.96562pt{K}}}}}w(\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}}))\big)=(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}.

Now suppose that
v\in(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. Since
v\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, it follows that
v\in g(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}})
for some
g\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.8371pt\kern 6.22598pt}}_{{\kern-9.5255pt{J}\kern 6.22598pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-5.8371pt\kern 6.22598pt}}_{{\kern-9.5255pt{J}\kern 6.22598pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-3.46373pt\kern 3.85262pt}}_{{\kern-6.24115pt{J}\kern 3.85262pt{K}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}G^{{\kern-2.36298pt\kern 2.75186pt}}_{{\kern-5.1404pt{J}\kern 2.75186pt{K}}}}
. Hence
g^{-1}(v)\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
. Since
v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, part (a) implies that
g^{-1}(v)\in g^{-1}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. By the definition of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, it is obvious that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})
. Thus,
g^{-1}(v)\in(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}})\cap\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
. Therefore,

v\in g(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}})\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}
.
Thus, we have established that
(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}
, and (b) is proved.

We prove (c). By (b), we have
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}=(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}})\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. Since
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Y}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
is an intersection of convex cones, it is itself a convex cone. By Theorem
1.65
(d), we know that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-9.20233pt{J}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-6.06354pt{J}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{X}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.01353pt{J}\kern 2.625pt}}}
is a cone. Thus,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-4.45836pt\kern 4.84724pt}}_{{\kern-8.14676pt{J}\kern 4.84724pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-2.60556pt\kern 2.99445pt}}_{{\kern-5.38298pt{J}\kern 2.99445pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\kern-1.75pt\kern 2.13889pt}}_{{\kern-4.52742pt{J}\kern 2.13889pt}}}
is a cone, since it is an intersection of two cones.
∎

8.4.

For any
\gamma\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
, define
\Omega_{J}(\gamma):=\{\,\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J\mid B_{J}(\gamma,\pi_{J}(\alpha))>0\,\}
.

Lemma 8.5
.

Let
\gamma\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
. Write
\gamma=\sum_{\beta\in\Pi\setminus J}c_{\beta}\pi_{J}(\beta)
where
c_{\beta}\geq 0
for all
\beta\in\Pi
. Then:

(a)

\Omega_{J}(\gamma)\subseteq\{\,\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J\mid c_{\alpha}>0,\pi_{J}(\alpha)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}\,\}
.

(b)

|\Omega_{J}(\gamma)|<\infty
.

(c)

\nu(\alpha,J)
is defined for all
\alpha\in\Omega_{J}(\gamma)
.

(d)

\Omega_{J}(\gamma)=\emptyset\iff\gamma\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
\alpha\in\Omega_{J}(\gamma)
.
Then

(8.5.1)

0<B_{J}(\gamma,\pi_{J}(\alpha))=\sum_{\beta\in\Pi\setminus J}c_{\beta}B(\pi_{J}(\beta),\pi_{J}(\alpha))

where
c_{\beta}\geq 0
for all
\beta\in\Pi\setminus J

Hence there exists
\beta\in\Pi\setminus J
such that
c_{\beta}>0
and

B(\pi_{J}(\beta),\pi_{J}(\alpha))>0
. By Proposition

6.10
, this implies that
\beta=\alpha
and the
component of
J\cup\{\alpha\}
containing
\alpha
is of finite
type, so
\pi_{J}(\alpha)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}
. Since

c_{\alpha}=c_{\beta}>0
, this proves (a). Part (b) follows since in
(a),
c_{\alpha}\neq 0
for only finitely many
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus J
.
Part (c) holds since
\nu(\alpha,J)
is defined for any
\alpha\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{{\mathrm{re}}}
.

We prove (d). Suppose that
\Omega_{J}(\gamma)={\emptyset}
.
Proposition
1.58
implies that
\gamma\in\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}})
,
where, by definition,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}}=\pi_{J}(\Pi\setminus J)
. By definition of
\Omega_{J}(\gamma)
, we have
B_{J}(\gamma,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}})\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\leq 0}
. Hence
\gamma\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}\cap\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}})=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
as required. Conversely, suppose that
\gamma\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
.
Then
\gamma\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-9.00787pt{J}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-5.92743pt{J}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-4.9163pt{J}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
, so
B_{J}(\gamma,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-9.86899pt{J}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-6.47188pt{J}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-5.3052pt{J}\kern 2.91667pt}}})\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\leq 0}
and
\Omega_{J}(\gamma)={\emptyset}
.
∎

Lemma 8.6
.

Assume that
(W,S)
is of finite rank. Fix a height function
\height\colon V\to\mathbb{R}
as in Proposition
2.46
(a).
Let
\gamma\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
.
If
|\Omega_{J}(\gamma)|>0
, then there exists some
\alpha\in\Omega_{J}(\gamma)
such that
\nu(\alpha,J)
exists. For any such
\alpha
, we have
\height(\nu(\alpha,J)\gamma)<\height(\gamma)
.

Proof.

By Proposition
8.5
, there exists some
\alpha\in\Omega_{J}(\gamma)
such that
\nu(\alpha,J)
is defined. Let
\nu(\alpha,J)=s_{1}s_{2}\dots s_{n}
be a reduced expression in
W
. For
i=1,\ldots,n
, let
\alpha_{i}\in\Pi
with
s_{\alpha_{i}}=s_{i}
, and define
\beta_{i}=s_{1}s_{2}\dots s_{i-1}(\alpha_{i})
. By Proposition
2.18
, we have

(8.6.1)

\gamma-\nu(\alpha,J)\gamma=B(\gamma,\alpha_{1}^{\vee})\beta_{1}+B(\gamma,\alpha_{2}^{\vee})\beta_{2}+\ldots+B(\gamma,\alpha_{n}^{\vee})\beta_{n}.

We have
\alpha_{i}\in J\cup\{\alpha\}
for all
i
.
If
\alpha_{i}\in J
, then
B(\gamma,\alpha_{i}^{\vee})=0
since
\gamma\in J^{\perp}
. If
\alpha_{i}=\alpha
, then

B(\gamma,\alpha_{i})=B_{J}(\gamma,\pi_{J}(\alpha))>0
since
\gamma\in J^{\perp}
and
\alpha\in\Omega_{J}(\alpha)
.
Since
\nu(\alpha,J)\in W_{J\cup\{\alpha\}}\setminus W_{J}
,
there must exist at least one
j
such that
\alpha_{j}=\alpha
.
We conclude that
B(\gamma,\alpha_{i})\geq 0
for all
i
and
that there exists at least one
j
such that
B(\gamma,\alpha_{j})=B(\gamma,\alpha)>0
. Hence, the right hand side of
(
8.6.1
)
is a non-zero, non-negative linear combination of positive roots. This implies that
\height(\gamma-\nu(\alpha,J)\gamma)>0

and so
\height(\gamma)>\height(\nu(\alpha,J)\gamma)
as required.
∎

Lemma 8.7
.

Assume that
(W,S)
is of finite rank.
Let
\gamma\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
. Then there exists a morphism
g=(K,w,J)
of
G
such that
