({\mathcal{V}}(g))(\gamma)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
.

Proof.

We first assume that
\Pi
is linearly independent. Fix a height function
\height\colon V\to\mathbb{R}
such that
\height(\alpha)=1
for all
\alpha\in\Pi
.
If
\Omega_{J}(\gamma)=\emptyset
, then take
g=(J,1_{W},J)\colon J\to J
. Now assume that
|\Omega_{J}(\gamma)|>0
.

Let us assume for the sake of contradiction that no such
g
existed. Then
\Omega_{K}(g(\gamma))\neq\emptyset
for all
K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)
and all morphisms
g\colon J\to K
in
G
.
Let
J_{0}:=J
. Applying Proposition
8.6
, there is a
a Brink-Howlett generator
h_{1}\colon J_{0}\to J_{1}
such that
\height(\gamma)>\height(h_{1}(\gamma))
. We have
h_{1}(\gamma)\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{h_{1}(J)}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-17.73936pt{h_{1}(J)}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{h_{1}(J)}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-17.73936pt{h_{1}(J)}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{h_{1}(J)}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-14.93289pt{h_{1}(J)}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{h_{1}(J)}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-14.93289pt{h_{1}(J)}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
. We must have
|\Omega_{h_{1}(J)}(h_{1}(\gamma))|>0
by the assumption at the start of this paragraph. Repeating this argument gives an infinite sequence

h_{i}=(J_{i},w_{i},J_{i-1})
of Brink-Howlett generators for
i=1,2,3,\ldots
such that

\height(\gamma)>\height(w_{1}(\gamma))>\height(w_{2}w_{1}(\gamma))>\height(w_{3}w_{2}w_{1}(\gamma))>\dots.

Since
\gamma\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
, we can write
\gamma=\pi_{J}(r_{U,J})
where
r_{U,J}\in\Phi^{+}
for some
(infinite index) corank
1
reflection overgroup
U
of
W_{J}
.
We may write

\gamma=\pi_{J}(r_{U,J})=r_{U,J}+c_{1}\alpha_{1}+\dots+c_{n}\alpha_{n}

where
c_{i}\in\mathbb{R}
and
\alpha_{i}\in J\subseteq\Pi
for each
i
. Consider the action of a Brink-Howlett morphism
h=(K,w,J)
on the above equation:

w(\gamma)=w(r_{U,J})+c_{1}w(\alpha_{1})+\dots+c_{n}w(\alpha_{n})

where
w(\alpha_{i})\in K\subseteq\Pi
for each
i
. Thus,

\height(c_{1}w(\alpha_{1})+\dots+c_{n}w(\alpha_{n})=c_{1}+\ldots+c_{n}=\height(c_{1}\alpha_{1}+\dots+c_{n}\alpha_{n}).

This tells us that
\height(\gamma)>\height(w(\gamma))
if and only if
\height(r_{U,J})>\height(w(r_{U,J}))
. Thus, the sequence

\height(\gamma)>\height(w_{1}(\gamma))>\height(w_{2}w_{1}(\gamma))>\height(w_{3}w_{2}w_{1}(\gamma))>\dots

implies

\height(r_{U,J})>\height(w_{1}(r_{U,J}))>\height(w_{2}w_{1}(r_{U,J}))>\height(w_{3}w_{2}w_{1}(r_{U,J}))>\dots

where
r_{U,J}
,
w_{1}(r_{U,J})
,
w_{2}w_{1}(r_{U,J})
,
\dots
are pairwise distinct positive roots.

By Proposition
2.46
, there exists some
\epsilon>0
such that
\height(\rho)>\epsilon\ell(s_{\rho})
for all
\rho\in\Phi^{+}
. Hence,

\height(r_{U,J})>\height(w_{i}w_{i-1}\dots w_{1}(r_{U,J}))>\epsilon\ell(s_{w_{i}w_{i-1}\dots w_{1}(r_{U,J})})\textrm{ for all }i.

This gives

\frac{1}{\epsilon}\height(r_{U,J})>\ell(s_{w_{i}w_{i-1}\dots w_{1}(r_{U,J})})\textrm{ for all }i

The above equation implies that there are infinitely many distinct reflections whose lengths are all less than
\frac{1}{\epsilon}\height(r_{U,J})
. This is impossible in a finite rank Coxeter system, a contradiction which completes the proof in the case
\Pi
is linearly independent.

Now consider the general case, with
\Pi
possibly linearly dependent. We sketch an argument involving compatibility of the construction of realized root systems of Brink-Howlett groupoids with lifts, to reduce to the case in which
\Pi
is linearly independent.
The argument is straightforward but somewhat lengthy, and we omit many routine details.

Consider a universal lift
L\colon\widehat{D}\to D
of
D=(V,B,\Phi,\Pi)
where
\widehat{D}=(\widehat{V},\widehat{B},\widehat{\Phi},\widehat{\Pi})
, defined as in
2.34
(we write here
\widehat{X}
instead of
\widetilde{X}
as in
2.34
, for typographical reasons).

In particular,
\widehat{\Pi}
is a basis of
\widehat{V}
, by definition of universal lifts. Denote the associated Coxeter system of the based root system
(\widehat{\Phi},\widehat{\Pi})
as
(\widehat{W},\widehat{S})
.
The canonical identification
\widehat{W}\to W
, denoted as
\widehat{w}\mapsto w
, makes
L\colon\widehat{V}\to V
a
W
-equivariant map and preserves bilinear forms. Also,
L
restricts to bijections
\widehat{\Phi}\to\Phi
and
\widehat{\Pi}\to\Pi

Many notions
X
we have defined for
(W,S)
from
(\Phi,\Pi)
on
(V,B)
have analogues, which we will denote
\widehat{X}
, defined in the same way for

(\widehat{W},\widehat{S})
from
(\widehat{\Phi},\widehat{\Pi})
on
(\widehat{V},\widehat{B})
.
For example, for any
J\subseteq\Phi
, we write
\widehat{J}=\{\,\alpha\in\widehat{\Phi}\mid L(\alpha)\in J\,\}
. Corresponding to the Brink-Howlett groupoid
G
of
(W,S)
,
there is a Brink-Howlett groupoid
\widehat{G}
of
(\widehat{W},\widehat{S})
with objects
\{\,\widehat{J}\mid J\in\operatorname{ob}(G)\,\}
and morphisms

(\widehat{K},\widehat{w},\widehat{J})
for morphisms
(K,w,J)
of
G
.

More importantly for the argument here, there is a realized signed groupoid set
(\widehat{G},\widehat{\Upsilon}_{X},\widehat{\mathcal{V}},\widehat{\iota}\,)
, where
X
denotes
R
,
M
or
P
, defined
in the same way as
(G,\Upsilon_{X},{\mathcal{V}},\iota)
.
The assumed validity of conditions
1.53
(i)–(iii) for
(G,V,\Pi)
implies their validity
for
(\widehat{G},\widehat{V},\widehat{\Pi})
; to prove this, one observes that, in Proposition
6.2
, if
K\subseteq\Pi
and
u\in\widehat{V}\setminus\widehat{V}_{\widehat{K}}
, then

L(u)\in V\setminus V_{K}={\emptyset}
, a contradiction if
K\in\operatorname{ob}(G)
. Hence we may identify
\widehat{\mathcal{V}}(\widehat{J}\,)=(\widehat{J}\,)^{\perp}\subseteq\widehat{V}
, for any
J\in\operatorname{ob}(G)
.

Henceforward, we identify
(W,S)
with
(\widehat{W},\widehat{S})
and
thereby
(\widehat{G},\widehat{\Upsilon}_{X})=(G,\Upsilon_{X})
, for
notational simplicity.
Let
J\in\operatorname{ob}(G)
. The projections on
(\widehat{J}\,)^{\perp}
and

J^{\perp}
are related by the formula
L\circ\pi_{\widehat{J}}=\pi_{J}\circ L\colon\widehat{V}\to J^{\perp}\subseteq V
. From the definitions, this
implies that
\iota_{J}=L\circ\widehat{\iota}_{J}\colon\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Upsilon^{{\kern-5.75003pt\kern 6.13892pt}}_{{\kern-9.43843pt{J}\kern 6.13892pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Upsilon^{{\kern-5.75003pt\kern 6.13892pt}}_{{\kern-9.43843pt{J}\kern 6.13892pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Upsilon^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\Upsilon^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}\to J^{\perp}
.
In particular,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}=L(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+})
. One also can check from the definitions that

L(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widetilde{J}\,}}}}\widetilde{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widetilde{J}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widetilde{J}\,}}}}\widetilde{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widetilde{J}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widetilde{J}\,}}}}\widetilde{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widetilde{J}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widetilde{J}\,}}}}\widetilde{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widetilde{J}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}})\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-9.41762pt{J}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-6.19965pt{J}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-5.11076pt{J}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
.

Now the lemma can be proved as follows. Let
\gamma\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}}+}
. Write
\gamma=L(\gamma^{\prime})
for some

\gamma^{\prime}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\widehat{\Upsilon}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
. By the case of linearly independent roots, there exists
\widehat{g}=(\widehat{K},\widehat{w},\widehat{J})
with
(\widehat{{\mathcal{V}}}(\widehat{g}))(\gamma^{\prime})\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widehat{K}\,}}}}\widehat{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widehat{K}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widehat{K}\,}}}}\widehat{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widehat{K}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widehat{K}\,}}}}\widehat{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widehat{K}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\widehat{K}\,}}}}\widehat{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-12.38892pt{\widehat{K}\,}\kern 5.55557pt}}}
.
Applying
L
to both sides shows that
({{\mathcal{V}}}(g))(\gamma)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.69
(d).

Let
\gamma\in{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-8.85509pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{J}}}}\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-7.9441pt{J}\kern 5.55557pt}}}}^{{\mathrm{im}},+}
, say
\gamma=r^{\prime}_{U,J}
where
U\in R_{J}
is an infinite index, corank one reflection overgroup of
W_{J}
. Recall that we assume that no simple root in
\Pi\setminus J
is joined in the Coxeter graph to infinitely many components of
J=J_{\mathrm{fin}}
. Then
r_{U,J}\in\Phi^{+}
and there exists a finite subset
\Pi^{\prime}
of
\Pi
such that

r_{U,J}\in W_{\Pi^{\prime}}
, and
J^{\prime}:=\Pi^{\prime}\cap J
is a union of components of
J
such that
J^{\prime}
contains every vertex of
J
which is joined in the Coxeter graph of
\Pi
to an element of
\Pi^{\prime}\setminus J
. In particular,
\Pi^{\prime}
contains every component of
J
with a vertex which is joined to
r_{U,J}
in the Coxeter graph of
U
(regarded as a graph on vertex set
J\cup\{r_{U,J}\}
).

Let
U^{\prime}
denote the standard parabolic subgroup of
U
with
\Pi_{U^{\prime}}=J^{\prime}\cup\{r_{U,J}\}

The component of
\{r_{U,J}\}\cup J
which contains
r_{U,J}
is of infinite type (since
[U:W_{J}]
is infinite) and it is contained in
\{r_{U,J}\}\cup J^{\prime}
. Hence it is the component of

\{r_{U,J}\}\cup J^{\prime}
which contains
r_{U,J}
. It follows that

U^{\prime}
is an infinite index, corank one reflection overgroup of
W_{J^{\prime}}
in
W_{\Pi^{\prime}}
.
We regard
W_{\Pi^{\prime}}
as the (finite rank) Coxeter group associated to a based root system
(\Phi^{\prime},\Pi^{\prime})
with simple roots
\Pi^{\prime}
in
(V^{\prime},B^{\prime})
where
V^{\prime}=(J\setminus J^{\prime})^{\perp}\subseteq V
and
B^{\prime}
is the restriction of
B
to a symmetric bilinear form on
V^{\prime}
.

Consider the component of
G^{\prime}
of the full Brink-Howlett groupoid of
W_{\Pi^{\prime}}
, such that
G^{\prime}
contains the object
J^{\prime}
. Then
G^{\prime}
has an associated
weakly realized root system
(G^{\prime},\Upsilon^{\prime},{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime},\iota^{\prime})
defined in the analogous way to
(G,\Upsilon,{\mathcal{V}},\iota)
. Conditions
1.53
(i)–(iii) for
(G,V,\Pi,J)
trivially imply the same conditions for
(G^{\prime},V^{\prime},\Pi^{\prime},J^{\prime})
. By Proposition
6.2
,

1.53
(i)–(iii) hold for
(G^{\prime},V^{\prime},\Pi^{\prime},K^{\prime})
for any object
K^{\prime}
of
G^{\prime}
. Hence the orthogonal projection
\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}
of
V^{\prime}
on
(K^{\prime})^{\perp}\cap V^{\prime}
is defined. In particular,
\pi^{\prime}_{J^{\prime}}\colon V^{\prime}\to(J^{\prime})^{\perp}\cap V^{\prime}
is defined.

Note that
\Pi_{U^{\prime}}=\{r_{U,J}\}\dot{\cup}J^{\prime}
where
r_{U,J}\in\Phi_{W^{\prime}}
, so
r_{U^{\prime},J^{\prime}}=r_{U,J}
.
By choice of
J^{\prime}
and Propositions
6.6
–
6.7
, we also have
r^{\prime}_{U,J}=\pi_{J}(r_{U,J})\in r^{\prime}_{U,J}+\operatorname{span}(J^{\prime})
, from which
r^{\prime}_{U,J}=\pi_{J^{\prime}}(r_{U,J})
. Hence
r^{\prime}_{U^{\prime},J^{\prime}}:=\pi^{\prime}_{J^{\prime}}(r_{U^{\prime},J^{\prime}})=r^{\prime}_{U,J}=\gamma
.

For any object
K
of
G^{\prime}
, we define

\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-13.15515pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-13.15515pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.63817pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.58817pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{\prime}=(K^{\prime})^{\perp}\cap V^{\prime}
for
K^{\prime}\in\operatorname{ob}(G^{\prime})
, with projection denoted
\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}\colon V^{\prime}\to\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-13.15515pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-13.15515pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.63817pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.58817pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{\prime}
.
There is an imaginary cone

{\mathcal{Z}}^{\prime}
for
(G^{\prime},\Upsilon^{\prime},{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime},\iota^{\prime})
, defined analogously to

{\mathcal{Z}}
for
(G,\Upsilon,{\mathcal{V}},\iota)
, with fundamental chamber at the object
K^{\prime}\subseteq\Pi^{\prime}
of
G^{\prime}
denoted as
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-9.77428pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-8.6854pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.72223pt}}}^{\prime}
.
From Lemma
8.7
, there is a morphism

g^{\prime}=(K^{\prime},w^{\prime},J^{\prime})
of
G^{\prime}
, with
w^{\prime}\in W^{\prime}
, such that
({\mathcal{V}}^{\prime}(g^{\prime}))(\gamma)\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-9.77428pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-8.6854pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.72223pt}}}^{\prime}
. That is,
w^{\prime}\gamma\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-9.77428pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-8.6854pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.72223pt}}}^{\prime}
.

Since
\Pi^{\prime}
is
B
-orthogonal to
J\setminus J^{\prime}
,
w^{\prime}\in W_{\Pi^{\prime}}
and
J=J^{\prime}\cup(J\setminus J^{\prime})
, there is a morphism
g:=(K,w^{\prime},J)
in
G

where
K:=K^{\prime}\cup(J\setminus J^{\prime})
. To complete the proof, it will therefore suffice to check that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-9.77428pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-8.6854pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.72223pt}}}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}}
.

We have
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-10.74129pt{K}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-7.14513pt{K}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-6.05624pt{K}\kern 2.72223pt}}}=\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})\cap(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}})
where
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}=\{\,v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-10.526pt{K}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-7.00902pt{K}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-5.95901pt{K}\kern 2.625pt}}}\mid B_{K}(v,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\,\}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}K^{{\kern-6.82088pt\kern 7.20976pt}}_{{\kern-11.83295pt{K}\kern 7.20976pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}K^{{\kern-6.82088pt\kern 7.20976pt}}_{{\kern-11.83295pt{K}\kern 7.20976pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}K^{{\kern-4.12318pt\kern 4.51207pt}}_{{\kern-7.84608pt{K}\kern 4.51207pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}K^{{\kern-2.83401pt\kern 3.2229pt}}_{{\kern-6.55692pt{K}\kern 3.2229pt}}}=\pi_{K}(\Pi\setminus K)
.

Similarly, write
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime}:=\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}(\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime})
.
Then
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-5.72922pt\kern 6.1181pt}}_{{\kern-13.37044pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 6.1181pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-3.42223pt\kern 3.81111pt}}_{{\kern-9.77428pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 3.81111pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}\,}}}}{\mathcal{K}}^{{\kern-2.33334pt\kern 2.72223pt}}_{{\kern-8.6854pt{K^{\prime}\,}\kern 2.72223pt}}}^{\prime}=\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime})\cap(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-8.3354pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-7.32428pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{\prime})
where
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-8.3354pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-7.32428pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{\prime}:=\{\,v\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-11.9885pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-11.9885pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-8.47151pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-7.42151pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{\prime}\mid B^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}(v,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime})\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}\,\}
with
B^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}
denoting the restriction of
B^{\prime}
to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-11.9885pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-11.9885pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.90282pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-8.47151pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{V}}^{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-7.42151pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.625pt}}}^{\prime}=(K^{\prime})^{\perp}\cap V^{\prime}
.

Note
\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime}\subseteq\Pi\setminus K
, since
K=K^{\prime}\dot{\cup}(J\setminus J^{\prime})

where
\Pi^{\prime}\cap(J\setminus J^{\prime})={\emptyset}
.
Let
\alpha\in\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime}
and write
\alpha^{\prime}:=\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}(\alpha)
.
Then
\alpha^{\prime}-\alpha\in\operatorname{span}(K^{\prime})
and
B(\alpha^{\prime},K^{\prime})\subseteq\{0\}
.
Hence
\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}\in\operatorname{span}(\Pi^{\prime})
where
\Pi^{\prime}\perp J\setminus J^{\prime}
.
This implies that
B(\alpha^{\prime},K)\subseteq\{0\}
and

\alpha^{\prime}-\alpha\in\operatorname{span}(K)
, so
\alpha^{\prime}=\pi_{K}(\alpha)
. We conclude that

(8.7.1)

\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}(\alpha)=\pi_{K}(\alpha)\text{ \rm if $\alpha\in\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime}$,}\quad\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}},\quad\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime})\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}}).

Note that
B(\alpha,K)\subseteq-\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
for
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus K
, by Theorem
2.9
. By Proposition
6.7
,
this implies
\pi_{K}(\alpha)\in\alpha+\operatorname{cone}(K)\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(\Pi)
for

\alpha\in\Pi\setminus K
. The same argument shows

(8.7.2)

\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}(\alpha)\in\alpha+\operatorname{cone}(K^{\prime})\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(\Pi^{\prime}),\qquad\alpha\in\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime}.

Let
v\in\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime})\cap(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-8.3354pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-7.32428pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{\prime})
.
Then we may write
v=\sum_{\alpha\in\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime}}c_{\alpha}\pi_{K^{\prime}}(\alpha)
where all
c_{\alpha}\geq 0
.
We also have
v\in V^{\prime}=(J\setminus J^{\prime})^{\perp}
, and, since

v\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-11.79404pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-8.3354pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-7.32428pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.52777pt}}}^{\prime}
,
v\in(K^{\prime})^{\perp}
and

B(v,\pi^{\prime}_{K^{\prime}}(\alpha))=B(v,\alpha)\leq 0
for all

\alpha\in\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime}
.

We are required to show that

v\in\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})\cap(-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}})
. From (
8.7.1
), we have
v\in\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-12.65515pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-8.87985pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K^{\prime}}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-7.71318pt{K^{\prime}}\kern 2.91667pt}}}^{\prime})\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-6.18059pt\kern 6.56947pt}}_{{\kern-11.19266pt{K}\kern 6.56947pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-3.69446pt\kern 4.08334pt}}_{{\kern-7.41736pt{K}\kern 4.08334pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}\Delta^{{\kern-2.52779pt\kern 2.91667pt}}_{{\kern-6.25069pt{K}\kern 2.91667pt}}})
, so it remains to prove
v\in-\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-5.31947pt\kern 5.70836pt}}_{{\kern-10.33154pt{K}\kern 5.70836pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-3.15001pt\kern 3.5389pt}}_{{\kern-6.87291pt{K}\kern 3.5389pt}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{K}}}}{\mathcal{C}}^{{\kern-2.13889pt\kern 2.52777pt}}_{{\kern-5.86179pt{K}\kern 2.52777pt}}}
. Equivalently, we have to show that
v\in K^{\perp}
and
B(v,\alpha)\leq 0
for all
\alpha\in\Pi\setminus K
.

We have
v\in K^{\perp}
since
K=K^{\prime}\cup(J\setminus J^{\prime})
,
v\in(K^{\prime})^{\perp}
and
v\in(J\setminus J^{\prime})\perp
. Also
B(v,\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime})\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\leq 0}
from above.
Also,

B(v,\Pi\setminus\Pi^{\prime})\subseteq B(\operatorname{cone}(\Pi^{\prime}),\operatorname{cone}(\Pi\setminus\Pi^{\prime}))\subseteq\operatorname{cone}(B(\Pi^{\prime},\Pi\setminus\Pi^{\prime}))\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\leq 0}

by (
8.7.2
) and Theorem
2.9
. This gives

B(v,\Pi\setminus K)\subseteq B(v,\Pi\setminus K^{\prime})\subseteq\mathbb{R}_{\leq 0}

since
\Pi\setminus K\subseteq\Pi\setminus K^{\prime}=(\Pi\setminus\Pi^{\prime})\cup(\Pi^{\prime}\setminus K^{\prime})
.
∎
